====================A00_鬱な朝☆.txt====================
Ema: Nn...nnnn...
*Ugh, my phone's alarm is always so loud... I mean, it kinda has to be. Anything less and I'd sleep right through it.*
Ema: Seven o'clock... I gotta get up... but...
Ema: Nnnn... I'm sleepy...
*It's no use. I keep telling myself to get up, but my eyelids are ignoring me and drifting shut.*
*Maybe I should go back to sleep after all...*
*I'm so drowsy... It's not like I NEED to get up, right?*
*My parents have already left for work. And they won't be home until late, either. If I skip school they won't know. Or at least they won't know right away.*
Ema: Ugh, I'm so sleepy... What... should I... do...?
*I've never let the temptation win before. I've always been a good student.*
*I know that if I give in once, it'll be easier to give in a second time, and then a third. Then before I know it, I'll be a complete failure of a human being...*
Ema: Sleepy or not, I gotta get up...
*Okay... I managed to get out of bed. Awesome. That's the first step. You're doing great, me.*
*Ugh... summoning the strength to get out of bed has used up all my energy.*
*I don't know if I can muster up the willpower to put on my uniform... Even just breathing is exhausting...*
*......*
*...*
Ema: Zzzzz...
Ema: ...
*As usual, my classmates haven't said a word to me.*
*To be fair, I'm not known for going out of my way to talk to them, either.*
*That's because...*
*Well...*
Female Student A: Ack!
*Oh, her mechanical pencil's rolling this way...*
*I stood up.*
Ema: I'll grab that for you. Here you go.
Female Student A: Eek! Uh, uh, um, umm...!
Ema: Oh.
*Here we go again. She snatched the pencil and ran away.*
*I went out of my way to get out of my seat and get it for her, too...*
Female Student B: She picked it up for you! You should at least say thank you!
Female Student A: B-But she's... y'know... terrifying!
Female Student B: True... When she stood up, I nearly jumped out of my skin!
Female Student A: RIGHT? For a girl, she's, like, unbelievably huge!
Ema: Ugh...
*You know I can totally hear you, right?*
*So I'm tall. So I have resting bitch-face. I was born this way. Honestly, how scary can I possibly be?*
*And yet they all walk on eggshells around me.*
Ema: (No... I need to look on the bright side...)
*I mean, things are way better than they used to be.*
*When I was little... well, you know how kids are. They'd kick me, and call me Bigfoot. The usual kind of stuff.*
*Once I hit puberty, well... The really overt bullying went away, but... Every time I think back to those days, I feel so sad...*
*...Yeah, compared to back then, this is nothing.*
*Since I enrolled here, this has happened a bunch of times. People are scared of me.*
*But as long as I don't go out of my way to interact with them, they basically just leave me alone completely.*
*...There are a lot of mature students here. I'm glad I worked hard to get into a high-ranking school.*
*This is the happiest my life has ever been... I think.*
Ema: (I just gotta keep my head down and try to stay invisible until I graduate...)
*......*
*...*
====================A01_巳紀の憤怒.txt====================
*Ding dong, ding dooong...*
*...Lunchtime.*
Ema: Whew... The one place I can actually relax...
*Thankfully, as usual, no one's out behind the school.*
*Probably because there are mosquitoes, and the ground is all muddy from the rain we've been having, but I digress.*
*To me, this place is paradise. As long as I can be alone in peace, nothing else matters.*
*Eating lunch with friends is a concept waaay out of my league.*
*I'm grateful just to get a break from all those searching looks.*
*Here, I can eat my convenience-store chocolate cream melon bun undisturbed. My one genuine happiness at school.*
Ema: Nom nom... mmmm... haah... The chocolate is melting in my mouth...
Female Student's Voice: Umm... I... I've had feelings for you all this time! Please go out with me, Miki-kun!
Ema: Hrrrk?!
*Someone's shouting. So much for my blissful silence!*
*There are people standing over by the storage shed. Who do they think they are, infringing on my solitude?*
Female Student's Voice: Could you, umm... give me an answer...?
Ema: ...
*Now I have to spy on them, obviously.*
*There's a girl with her hair done up in buns standing beside the shed. I take it she's the one who shouted that confession just now?*
Miki-kun(?): ...
*In that case, the blond guy facing her must be the recipient of said confession...*
*I'm pretty sure she said his name was "Miki-kun" or someth—*
Ema: I can't. You're not the one.
Girl with Buns: I'm not... the one...?
Ema: Okay. I'll drop the subject for today. But no matter how long it takes, I'm going to make you fall for me, Miki-kun!
Ema: I'm going to do my very best... until you fall in love with me!
Miki: Do your best? Doing what, exactly?
Girl with Buns: Huh? Well, umm... I'll do my best to mold myself into the kind of girl you can fall for, I guess...?
Miki: Not possible.
Girl with Buns: What? I can't...?
Ema: Not possible
*? Ouch! Talk about harsh...*
*This "Miki" guy is stone cold! What a jerk!*
Miki: You aren't my muse.
Girl with Buns: Your muse...?
Miki: You're always looking at me. I've known this for a while now. But can I just ask: Why were you always making that face at me?
Girl with Buns: Making a face? What face?
Miki: I dunno. You always made this weird face when you looked at me.
Girl with Buns: What? Ummm...
Miki: You know, the thing where you smile and pout your lips at the same time.
Girl with Buns: Um... duck face? Is that what you mean? Oh, I... I was just trying to look cute for you...
Miki: I see. So when women make this face on TV or in the 45-degree- angle selfies they post to their blogs, it's because their crush is watching?
Girl with Buns: ...
Miki: I go to America, come back, and suddenly Japanese girls are sex- obsessed and desperate, is that it?!
Girl with Buns: ...
Ema: I'm sorry, I'm a little confused. Are you mad about something...?
Miki: Am I mad about something? You bet I'm mad! Everyone looks the same! I'm sick of it!
Girl with Buns: Okay... Now I'm even more confused.
Miki: I can't find my muse, okay?! She's NOWHERE. Well, no, I mean, she's somewhere. I saw her once, but haven't been able to track her down since...
Ema: Oh, it's you...
Ema: Uhhh...
*Crap! I was too focused on trying to make sense of his nonsense and I let my guard down!*
Miki: What are you doing here...?
Ema: What do you mean, what am I...?
*Why does he look so shocked to see me? I mean, I guess it's kinda weird for a girl to be out here, but surely not THAT weird, right?*
*Or, wait... Maybe he's mad at me...?*
Ema: It... It's not what you think! I was just passing by...
Miki: Passing by...?
Ema: I wasn't trying to spy on you or anything...
Miki: So you heard our little conversation, huh?
Ema: EEEK?!
*Oh god! I've just dug my own grave!*
Ema: I'm sorry! I'll get out of your sight now!
Miki: Wh—Hey! Wait!
Ema: Uh... Nope!
*Time to run like my life depends on it!*
Miki: Wait! Please! Don't go!
Ema: EEEEK! Why is he chasing meee?!
*He's running after me with a weird look on his face.*
*Was what I did so bad that he feels the need to hunt me down?! Is this what I get for spying?!*
*Honestly, after having to see a rejection that savage, I'm a victim more than anything else!*
Girl with Buns: HOLD IT, ASSHOLE! We ain't done here! You think I'm sex-obsessed and desperate, huh?! HUH?!
Miki: C-Cut it out! Let... Let go of me! I need to talk to that girl!
Ema: EEEEEEEEK!
*I can hear them yelling at each other somewhere behind me...*
*After all this, they're still fighting? How much energy do these people have?!*
*For the time being, I need to get as far away from them as I can!*
*......*
*...*
====================A02_君は僕のミューズ.txt====================
Ema: Haah...haah...haah... Uggghh... That was terrifying...
*Good thing I found somewhere to hide...*
*No! Not good! My precious lunchtime respite is ruined! If I'd known this was going to happen, I would've skipped school.*
*While I was running, I heard people saying things like "Look at that scowl! That girl's about to run someone down!"*
*It seems like no matter what I do, people are gonna get the wrong idea about me.*
Ema: Haah... I still kinda feel bad for that girl who got rejected...
*Her duck face totally backfired on her. I guess some guys aren't fans...*
*But still, she was doing it to make herself look cute. People ought to appreciate the level of effort that goes into something like that...*
*I know I can't do a duck face, even if I wanted to. The corners of my mouth wouldn't curl up like that.*
Ema: Nnn... Nnnnn...!
*Let's see... Am I doing it right?*
*Smile, then pout my lips out a bit... Like this?*
Ema: Nnn? Nnnnn?!
*Whoa... I think I'm getting it! I'm curious to see what it makes my face look like...*
*If I use the front facing camera on my phone—*
Ema: Eeeegh?!
Miki: There you are! Why are you doing this?!
Ema: EEEEK! It's not what you think! I just felt like making a duck face!
Miki: Why do you keep running from me?! Do you know how long I've been searching for you?!
Ema: Oh... THAT'S what you're mad about?
*It doesn't make a lot of difference what the specific reason for his anger is, really... And... he's been searching for me? Just how persistent IS this guy?*
*It seems like he's REALLY determined to get me back for spying.*
*People love finding a scapegoat for their problems. I see it all the time on the internet. Maybe this guy wanted someone to blame for things, too...*
Miki: I knew it. It was you all along! You're my MUSE!
Ema: Your muse...?
Miki: Yes! You are the MUSE I've been searching for!
*He gave me a broad smile, and launched into a rambling speech that I followed precisely none of.*
*I don't understand a word, but who cares about that? I'm just glad he's not mad at me anymore.*
*And what's a "muse," anyway? Is it just a quirk in how he speaks or something?*
Miki: Anyway, please just hear me out!
Ema: Whoa!
Ema: E-EEEEK?!
*What the... Did he just grab my shoulders and lean in...?!*
*Is that how he interacts with strangers? Hasn't he ever heard of personal space?*
Ema: Uh, listen...
Miki: I knew it. I was right. You're the one...
*He fell silent and drew close, staring at my face intently.*
*I was about to object to this, but the intensity of his gaze drew me in, and I suddenly couldn't get the words out.*
Miki: I knew it the moment I first laid eyes on you.
Ema: L-Listen, uh, I don't understand what you're...
Miki: Now, up close, I'm more certain than ever. You are, without a doubt, MY MUSE!
*There he goes with the muse thing again... Is this a word everyone's supposed to know offhand? Is it weird that I don't?*
*Wait... Does "muse" mean what I think it means...?*
*If it does, this guy is being incredibly weird!*
Ema: Uh, listen... I'm not a muse.
Miki: What?! Why would you say such a thing?!
Ema: Well, I've never been very good at comedy, so...
Miki: MUSE, not AMUSE! Haah... What a relief!
Ema: First you yell at me, and now you're all giddy? You're big on the emotional whiplash, huh?
*Great. I'm caught in the orbit of this space case. Maybe I can sneak away while he's gripped by emotion...*
Miki: It must have been Fate that brought me back to this country! All so that I could meet you!
Ema: EEEEEEK!!!
*Just as I was about to try another escape, he drew in even closer to me!*
*And with a huge smile, too. It's as if he doesn't even care that I'm freaking out!*
Miki: Haah... Your beauty becomes more unique and luminous the longer I look... I knew it! I was right to trust my intuition.
Ema: I... I don't like that!
*Desperate to get him out of my personal bubble, I started pushing back against his large frame.*
*Unfortunately, as big as he was, and as flustered as I was, it... didn't really work. Like, at all. He didn't budge.*
Ema: Ah! Aaaaagh! ...Mmph?!
*When I decided I needed to use more force, I forgot to make sure I didn't use too much of it... and now I've slammed my face into his chest.*
Ema: Huh...? You want a hug? So you agree to be my muse, then!
Ema: ...Huh? What? WHAAAT?!
*This is going from bad to worse. This guy has chosen to just keep digging us further and further down the misunderstanding hole.*
*Even though I want to correct his increasingly wild interpretations, I can't get the words out. My head is a mess. I've never had this much physical contact with a guy before.*
Ema: You're smiling! Does that mean you've come around?
Ema: What? No... That's not it...
Miki: YES! MY MUSE!
Ema: Ngh... GAAAAAAHHHHH!!!
*Now he's hugging me even tighter! And... breathing heavier?!*
*My face has got to be bright red. I can't believe a complete stranger would hug me. Nope... I can't even process this.*
Ema: S-STOP!
Miki: Whoa...!
*Frantically, I pushed him out of my personal bubble.*
*Good grief. Who IS this guy?*
Ema: Th-That's quite enough, thank you! L-Listen, I can see that you're very attractive, and I'm sure you're a real ladies' man—
Miki: Uh, no, I wouldn't say I'm that popular...
Ema: —but that doesn't mean I can open the family coffers for you!
Miki: C-Coffers...?
*He looks completely confused, but I'm sure that's just because he's a good actor. He's a scam artist, after all.*
*I have to push past my fear and speak my mind. Otherwise I bet he'll start rambling nonsense and make some other totally confusing, distracting move on me!*
Ema: M-My parents are nice folks. They'd probably do a lot for me if I asked them to. But they won't just give their money to some guy.
Miki: No, no! That's not my intention in the least!
Ema: Then what is it? Muse this, muse that! Putting your arms around a girl you don't know... Is this part of your religion or something?!
*I put on my best glare. I don't have much experience with actually being angry, though, so I have no idea if I've got the look right.*
*Usually I don't have to make any effort at all before the other person gets frightened off...*
*Sheesh. Just when I find someone who ISN'T afraid of me... Why'd he have to turn out to be a total nutcase?!*
*Oh! I could do the duck face thing from earlier. He really hates that, right?  I bet that'll do the trick and get rid of him.*
Ema: Nnnnn!
Miki: Uh, listen... Y- You see, by 'muse' I mean 'goddess'.
Ema: Nngh... Goddess? ...Me?
*He thinks I'M a goddess? This is getting more and more ridiculous.*
*All my life, I've only ever been called things like "Beanpole" or "Stretch"... you know, the most ungirly nicknames imaginable. I'd even accept "Omelette."*
*But "goddess"...?*
Ema: Oh, do you mean like, that monument in New York? I guess I can see why you'd...
Miki: No, no! How can I put it... Haven't you ever heard the term 'muse' used in the fashion industry?
Ema: Fashion...? If you're trying to put together some weird prank, I already told you I'm not good at...
Miki: This has absolutely nothing to do with comedy! In fashion design, 'muse' is a term for the person who motivates your art!
Ema: Uhhh... I think I'm more likely to deflate than motivate any art...
Miki: Nonsense! My desire to create is already beginning to gestate! Even this conversation is doing wonders! My mind is filled with new design ideas!
Ema: Design...?
Miki: If I close my eyes, I can picture you, with all your quirky charm, wearing MY CLOTHES!
Ema: S-Stop that! I don't want to be part of your comedy skit!
Miki: Don't worry, there's nothing amusing about it. Oh...! Now I just need to know your size...
Ema: My size...?
Miki: Sorry, but I need to measure you! Just for a sec!
Ema: M-Measure me? How?
Miki: Like THIS!
Ema: GyaaaaAAAAAAAAHHHH!!!
*As soon as the words left his mouth, his arms were around me again!*
Ema: I... I knew you were a pervert!! How much do you want?! How much do I have to pay to get rid of you?!
Miki: No, no, I'm just taking your waist measurements. Hmm... Okay, got it!
Ema: Haah... Haah...
Miki: An estimate won't really cut it, though... It's my first time working with someone of this stature, so I really ought to get the exact measurements...
*Just when it seemed like we were getting somewhere, he went back into his own little mumbling world.*
*What a waste of good genetics... I bet his parents thought they'd hit the jackpot, but instead they got Rain Man for clothes. Rain- Coat Man.*
*Ding dong, ding dooong...*
Ema: Oh...!
*That's the five minute bell. Lunch is almost over. Thanks to this weirdo, I lost all of my precious break time...*
Ema: Well... umm... I'd better go back to class...
Miki: Yeah, I guess so. Sorry for freaking you out. I just REALLY want to make clothes for you, that's all.
Ema: You want to... make clothes for me...?
*As I was trying to process this sudden declaration, he started scribbling something on a piece of paper,*
*then pushed it into my hands. Do I even get a say in anything happening here?*
Ema: Is this... your phone number... and your address?
Miki: Well, I want to make clothes for you, so please get in touch with me if you decide to try it out. Or come see me in person, if you want.
Ema: Yeah, uh, about that...
Miki: If you give me the chance, I'll make you some clothes that'll bring out your individuality PERFECTLY! You'll see!
Ema: Right...
Miki: Well, my sweet muse, I'll see you around!
*...*
Ema: ...Uhhh...
*He was beaming with delight as he ran off... Is that what people refer to as a "dazzling smile"?*
*In any case, I'm relieved he's gone. But what do I do with this note?*
*Looking at it, I can tell that he's serious. He wouldn't have given me his contact info otherwise.*
Ema: Clothes... for me...
*At home, sweatpants are my go-to choice, and I've never really thought about dressing up.*
*Besides...*
Ema: (Mom's always talking about how impossible it is to find cute clothes for someone as tall as me... Haah...)
*It's a little too late to start worrying about fashion. I should go back to class...*
*......*
*...*
====================A03_才人襲来.txt====================
Ema: Whew...
*Class is finally over. The prospect of walking home alone is already making me feel more relaxed.*
*All in all, today was pretty peaceful. Nobody bullied me or anything.*
*Well... I did have to cope with that weird guy at lunch.*
Ema: That takes care of that.
???: Hey, you! What was that you just threw away?
Ema: Huh...?
*I think I just heard a voice... Who's there?*
???: You there! Yes, you!  !
Ema: Wait, where're you…
???: ...
*An attractive, yet extremely intimidating guy is standing right behind me.*
*How long has he been following me?! His uniform matches mine, so I guess he's an academy student...*
Ema: E-Excuse me, but how do you know my name?
Scary-looking Guy: Please. I did my research on you a looong time ago.
Ema: R-Research...?
Scary-looking Guy: Listen here, you twerp. Littering isn't cool. Someone's gonna have to clean that up, y'know.
Ema: Y-You're right... I'm really sorry.
Scary-looking Guy: What the... HEY! This is Miki's contact info! Why would you throw this away?!
Ema: Um... Well... I...
*The look on his face tells me I'm being hardcore judged right now...*
*Littering was lousy of me to start with, but I guess it's even worse that I just left someone's personal information out where anyone could find it, so...*
*Wait... did he just say "Miki"?*
Ema: By Miki, you mean that blond guy? Really tall?
Scary-looking Guy: Pot calling the kettle black, much?
Ema: Nngh!
*Y-You didn't have to put it like that...*
Scary-looking Guy: What, did I hit a nerve?
Ema: N-No, I don't care...
Scary-looking Guy: Liar. I saw that look you gave me. Way to make your resting bitch- face even worse.
Ema: Nngghh!!!
*Has this guy even HEARD of manners? I mean, yeah, I'm tall, and yeah, I look bitchy...*
Scary-looking Guy: Let me guess: you don't like people talking about your looks?
Ema: E-Exactly... I'd prefer it if people didn't mention my appearance...
*I didn't ASK to be this tall. I didn't ASK to be called "Jumbo" or "Bigfoot" or whatever...*
*But I've been putting up with it ever since I was a young kid, and I've learned that getting defensive at the start saves time later on.*
Scary-looking Guy: Well, no offense and all, but... Man, you are HUGE. And your face is... really unique.
Ema: S-Seriously...?!
*I've met people who're deliberately obtuse, but SERIOUSLY. I JUST told him I don't want to hear it!*
*How can I get him to grasp that?*
Scary-looking Guy: Heheh.
Ema: Whoops... Conversation got a little side-tracked there. So, why did you try to ditch Miki's contact info?
Ema: Well, umm... How do I put this... I've got more than enough to cope with already, so...
Scary-looking Guy: I always thought you were a miserable little raincloud, but this is next level... You need to fix that personality of yours. It's annoying.
Ema: Says you...
*Does he really think I'll just drop everything and change who I am because he says so? Something tells me this guy isn't very smart.*
*Wait... Maybe he has a history of mentally conditioning people or something...*
*Nnnn... I'm getting creeped out just thinking about it...*
Scary-looking Guy: Why are you shaking like that? Whatever. Let's just go.
Ema: G-Go? Go where?
Scary-looking Guy: To Miki's.
Ema: What? I don't understand.
Scary-looking Guy: Miki explained it to you, right? He wants to make clothes for you.
Ema: Okay, but... I have plans today, so...
Scary-looking Guy: Liar. As if a loner like you would have plans.
Ema: Nngh...!
*I mean, he's right. I AM a loner. But assuming things about my life is incredibly rude!*
*How does he know I don't have plans? Maybe I'm going birdwatching or something!*
*...I'm not going birdwatching. BUT I COULD BE!*
Ema: Wh-Whatever. I'm not going anywhere with you. I'm going home.
Scary-looking Guy: HEY! Listen when I'm talking, you sow!
Ema: Gaahh?! THAT'S MY ARM!
Scary-looking Guy: If asking nicely won't work, then I'll have to convince you the hard way!
Ema: GyaaaaAAAAAAHHHHH!!!
Scary-looking Guy: When people hear a scream, they typically think someone's in trouble. With that yell, though, they'll think they're the ones who need to worry.
Ema: Aaagghhh...!
*What does he want me to do?*
*And another thing—why exactly does he have to shove me against the fence like this?*
Scary-looking Guy: ...
Ema: Wh... What's your deal? Did this Miki-san guy tell you to bring me to him?
Scary-looking Guy: Excuse me? I'm doing this because I feel like it. Especially after everything Miki told me about you.
Ema: You just randomly decided to do this?
Scary-looking Guy: I've been curious about you myself for a while now. Like, 'Whoa, there goes Bitchy Bigfoot.' 
Ema: L-Like I said before, could you maybe stop prodding at my emotional bruises?
Scary-looking Guy: But as it turns out, you're just the kind of woman that Miki wants to make clothes for. So of course I can't remain detached, now can I?
Ema: Yeah, uh, if you think this is the way to make me change my mind, think again. I told you, I'm not going.
Scary-looking Guy: In that case, I'll spread a nasty rumor about how you and Miki are dating.
Ema: What?
Scary-looking Guy: A lot of girls are pretty hung up on him, you know... If a rumor like that got around, a whole bunch of people might suddenly have it out for you.
Ema: Th... That's horrible...
*This is ridiculous!*
*Not only is he threatening me, but he's using his own friend as the threat, too!*
*I can't give in to that kind of bullying!*
Ema: S-Suit yourself. Nobody's going to believe that a hot guy like that would go out with a girl like me.
Scary-looking Guy: So the miserable little raincloud is a coward too, huh? You just get worse and worse, don't you?
Ema: It's the truth. I'm not even considered female.
Scary-looking Guy: Not female? I highly doubt that. You're plenty female.
Ema: Eeaaghh!
*What the... Goosebumps...?*
*His lips are so close to my ear... All he has to do is whisper, and a shiver runs down my spine...*
Scary-looking Guy: Did you just shudder? What, does it turn you on, being called female?
Ema: I... I'm not... turned on...
Scary-looking Guy: You are, aren't you? Look at that...
Ema: Eeek! Nnn...!
Scary-looking Guy: That's a very female reaction indeed.
Ema: Wh... Whatever! Enough! Please, just stop!
Scary-looking Guy: Hmm... Should I...? Then again, no matter what your gender is, your face stays just as alarming.
Ema: Th... That's so rude!
Ema: Okay then... Two can play at that game!
Scary-looking Guy: Oh yeah? What do you have in mind?
Ema: I'll tell people that Miki-san is going out with YOU, not me. Spice things up with a little BM!
Scary-looking Guy: BM? Don't you mean BL? BM means... you know what, never mind.
Ema: R-Right. BL. Scaaary BL. You'll transform into a divine creature that transcends gender... with really big hands.
Scary-looking Guy: Divine creature? Literally none of that is true. You're just parroting memes from the internet.
Scary-looking Guy: ANYWAY. You get that going to Miki's means he'll make you clothes, right? Who hates free clothes? What've you got against this?
Ema: There's no such thing as a free lunch! Besides, he'll probably start charging me after the first time!
Scary-looking Guy: You know this isn't a video game, right? Jesus... You're self- abasing AND paranoid? What are you, a smorgasbord of awful?
Ema: You're pretty awful yourself.
Scary-looking Guy: SHUT UP! You're coming with me, you damn SOW!
Ema: GyaaaAAAAaaaaAAAAHHHH!!!
*When I tried to refuse, he yanked hard on my arm.*
*Why is this guy such an asshole?! What did I do to deserve being treated like this?!*
Scary-looking Guy: Oh shit... The second I said 'sow', I got chills. Man, I can't get enough of your pain and misery.
Ema: Ugh...! Aaaagghhh...!
*Normally when I see someone smile I feel warm and contented inside, but all he makes me feel is dread...*
*Only movie villains have smiles like that!*
Ema: What's wrong? You went quiet all of a sudden. Did you change your mind?
Ema: I... I'll go... Just please don't cut off my fingers...!
Scary-looking Guy: What? What are you TALKING about? Seriously, we just want to make clothes for you. End of story.
Ema: ...
*This is some kind of tailoring scam... Maybe that's what's popular with criminals right now...*
*God, why did they have to target ME, of all people...?*
*......*
*...*
====================A04_エマの身長は173.txt====================

*Still, I find it hard to believe that Saito-san does any kind of domestic duties.*
*Since my parents are hardly ever home, I tend to do a bit of cleaning after school, but Saito-san seems like the more thorough type.*
*I know I definitely couldn't cook. It's too scary.*
*Oh, I know. When I get home, I'll write "lunch: homemade bento" in my blog entry for the day.*
*You know, when they were talking about remodeling, I thought they meant something terrifying. But if THIS is what they meant, well, I guess it's not so bad.*

====================C12a_存在の耐えられない重さ.txt====================
Ema: So, uh... Have you ever been to one of these night fairs before?
Miki: Not since I was a kid, no. I do remember being here in summertime at some point, but typically I spent summer break in America.
Ema: Same here. Last time I came to one of these, it was with my parents, and I was just a kid... So I guess neither of us has much experience with these, do we?
Miki: On the contrary! My old night fair memories are engraved in my mind! Tonight, I'm going to prove to you that I can show you a good time!
Ema: Okay...
*Whoa. His grip on my hand just got even tighter... You really don't have to get so worked up about it, dude...*
Miki: Uhhh... Okay, let's give that thing a try! The, uhh, you know...
*"Oh, you mean die-cutting?"*
Miki: Die-cutting... That sounds like fashion terminology.
Ema: It does...? Well, I guess you DO use needles for both, so yeah...
Miki: Y-Yeah, I knew that! Obviously! You use a needle to make little cuts, right?
Ema: Yeah, you do. It requires a lot of minute, detailed handiwork, so I bet you'd be pretty good at it, huh?
Miki: Of course I'm good at it! I'll show you just how minute I am!
Ema: ...Is that supposed to be a good thing...?
*...*
*As it turned out, Miki-san was in fact perfectly suited to the task. But then the guy running the die-cutting stand started criticizing him...*
*The mold wasn't the only thing getting needled, in other words...*
*Then Miki-san started screaming in Spanish for some reason, and the guy backed off... I'm pretty sure everyone in the vicinity is afraid of us now...*
Miki: Yes! At last, I've won the die-cutting mold...!
Ema: Do you even have a use for a vaguely airplane-shaped mold...?
Miki: I could pour gold into it and make accessories! Besides, I was able to prove that I was in the right! Best possible outcome!
Ema: Well, yours WAS the best one. It was worth the hour of effort it took.
Miki: What? An hour...? It took me that long?
Ema: Well, you made full use of the time limit in the advanced course.
Miki: Crap!!! I can't believe I wasted so much time on something so pointless!!!
Ema: You don't have to tear your hair out over it like that. I was impressed by how focused you were.
Miki: I didn't bring you here to impress you with THAT! What am I doing?! I need to make up for all that lost time!
Ema: Okay then, let's move on.
Miki: Yeah! Let's have some real fun! Ooh, people are crouching down over there. What are they doing?
Ema: Why don't we go find out?
*...*
Miki: Are those... turtles?
Ema: It's a turtle scooping game. I didn't know such a thing existed... Wow...
Miki: Do you like turtles?
Ema: I don't know if I like them, but I do envy them. If I had a shell like that, I could live my life slow and steady, just the way I want.
Miki: In that case, I could make you some clothes with a turtle motif. Yeah, something with a hood... Make it skin-tight so that when you lean forward, it peels back, exposing the tip of your head...
Ema: I'm not sure you realize how lewd that sounds...
Miki: Hmm... I was trying to think of the next step for my fashion, but I guess it's back to the drawing board... Man, this turtle scooping game sure is popular.
Ema: At this rate, we'll never get a turn. Let's go check out something else.
Miki: You're right... Oh, there's darts over there! Man, these night fairs have everything.
Ema: I've never played darts... Have you?
Miki: Back in America, I used to play all the time with my friends. Since we're here, why don't we check it out? It looks open.
Ema: Sure. Oh, it looks like you can win prizes based on your score. Oh! A sea anemone phone strap...!
Miki: Aha! So that's my lady's desire, eh? Then I'll just have to get it for her!
Ema: ...
*I just like how fluffy it looks, that's all...*
*...*
Miki: Nngh...! I can't believe I managed to win first prize 5 out of 5 times...! Why can't I win third prize?!
Ema: Hey, uhh, maybe we should stop... The guy in charge looks like he's gonna cry...
*...*
Miki: Whew! Good thing the guy in charge was so understanding! I'm lucky he was willing to accept 5 first prize vouchers for the strap!
Ema: He seemed pretty happy about it, actually...
Miki: So anyway, this is for you!
Ema: Are you sure?
Miki: Of course! I went to all that effort just for you!
Ema: Thank you! I love it! Wow... It's even fluffier than I thought...! And it's so detailed, right down to the basal disc in the middle.
Miki: Hehehe! I'm just glad you like it. Man, I'm actually kinda thirsty...
Ema: They're selling shaved ice. Wanna get some? Oh, but... they're kinda big, though.
Miki: In that case, it's the perfect size for sharing.
Ema: True. If I ate a whole one, it'd probably make me sick.
Miki: All right then, let's split one... But how exactly do we split it?
Ema: What do you mean, how? Common sense says down the middle. Let's just cut it with the spoon.
Miki: But the shaved ice is piled kinda high... If we do that, there's a chance it could topple over.
Ema: In that case, how about you eat the top half and I eat the bottom half?
Miki: No, no, no! The top half is the best part! It would offend my sensibilities as a gentleman to take it for myself!
Ema: Okay, then can I have the top half?
Miki: Wait... But that would mean I'd be eating the ice that touched your spoon, which came into contact with your mouth, and I'm sure you don't want that...
Ema: ...We'll take strawberry flavor, please.
Miki: Whoa! Completely ignored! That's my muse, all right!
Ema: Haha...!
*I can't stop smiling.*
*We're just walking around the night fair, but I've never really done anything like this before... It's so new to me...*
Ema: Whew... Reminds me of Halloween. We'd be out all night...
Ema: Yeah, I guess that's the American equivalent. Personally, this is my first time really letting loose.
Miki: Well, I'm glad you're having fun!  But the night is yet young! Where shall we go next?
Ema: Hmmm... I think we've made the rounds... Oh! We haven't tried that yet!
Miki: What's that say...? 'Snake Woman Show'...?
Ema: S-Snake woman? That sounds intense. Like something out of an RPG.
Miki: Yeah, they certainly are similar to harpies.
Ema: What...? I should think harpies are more bird than snake...
Miki: But aren't snakes a little too scary for girls?
Ema: Not necessarily. They're just reptiles, after all. They're nowhere near as scary as humans.
Miki: That was silly of me. I know my muse would never get flustered over a snake or two.
Ema: Well, shall we go in?
*...*
Ema: Wh... whoa...!
*There's an old wom—I mean, a lady—with scaly skin flicking her tongue... She's hissing out her life story, apparently...*
Ema: Urgh...
*Oh no... This is actually... scarier than I thought it'd be... Wait... Isn't she technically a monster...?*
*I just remembered... Monsters are the one thing I can't handle...! Even I'm not desperate enough to make friends with someone like THAT...!*
Miki: Nnn... Nnnnnn...! Poor thing...! Sold to a freak show as a kid... Waaahh...!
Ema: Y-Yeah...
*Miki-san seems to be empathizing with her... Crap... I can't ask him to leave when he's so engrossed in the story...*
Ema: ...
*Looking at her is freaking me out, so maybe I should cling to Miki-san... He's so caught up in it, he probably won't notice...*
Ema: Haahh... What a story. I was expecting something scary, but it was actually really touching. I'm so weak for that kinda stuff...
Ema: I... see...
Miki: That poor thing and her controlling parents... Argh! It didn't even happen to me, and I'm still upset!
Ema: ...
Miki: What did you think? You were quiet the whole time. Were you super into it like I was?
Ema: N-Not really...
Miki: Wait... You look kinda pale.
Ema: I... I'm okay. It's just temporary...
Miki: ...Were you... scared?
Ema: Um... a little...
Miki: Nngh...! God... I'm so STUPID...!
*Oh... Oh no. There he goes, blaming himself again...*
Ema: B-But um, it's okay because your chest helped me.
Miki: My... chest?
Ema: I know I should have asked first, but you were really focused, so I just... borrowed it for a bit. I'm sorry... It helped me stay calm, though.
Miki: Why would you apologize for that? Of course you're allowed to borrow my chest if you want.
Ema: Okay... Thanks. I appreciate you saying that. When I'm with you, I don't need to overthink anything... It's a huge relief.
Miki: You don't need to... overthink anything? What do you mean by that?
Ema: Well, I know you only see me as the girl who wears your clothes...
Miki: What...?
Ema: I'm just saying, I know I can touch you and it won't make things weird.
*Even that one time when I thought he was going to force himself on me, all he did was tell me he wanted to put me in a doll case...*
Miki: ...
Ema: I mean, we've held hands countless times tonight with no issues. With most guys, there'd be this weird sexual tension or something. Like, I bet if Saito-san were here instead, he'd—
Miki: Why are you bringing Saito up right now?
Ema: Huh...? Um, are you mad about something...?
Miki: ...No. Sorry, but do you mind if I go to the restroom real fast?
Ema: Sure...
*Why did he get upset when I mentioned Saito-san? Did they get into a fight while I was gone?*
*There's so much I don't understand about this guy...*
*...*
====================C12b_バイト帰りは会話したくない派だった.txt====================
Ema: Uggghhh... Free at last...
Saito: How can you possibly be THAT tired...? C'mon, what kinda model plods along like that?! Stand up straight! Brisk, purposeful steps!
Ema: But I'm tireddd... Can't you cut me a break...?
Saito: So, how was it? Starting to get the hang of it?
Ema: Not at all... I just keep thinking about what an idiot I am.
Saito: Everybody struggles at first. I'm sure you'll do better tomorrow.
Ema: Maybe... I sure hope so, anyway...
Saito: Wait... You're not gonna beg to quit?
Ema: Oh, uhh... no...
*I'm uneasy about it, but as of right now I don't really hate it or want to quit.*
*Maybe it's because everyone there is nice, if a little out there. I'm not sure it's helping me gain confidence, but I DO feel like being there will help me change somehow...*
Ema: ...
Saito: What?
Ema: Oh, uh, it's nothing...
*In a lot of ways, it was maybe kinda interesting to see a different side to Saito-san...*
*I'd sooner die than admit it, though.*
*Oh, that reminds me...*
Ema: So you're the producer at that restaurant?
Saito: Yeah, something like that, I guess. Nothing huge, though.
Ema: But you must be pretty serious about it if you're making them call you producer, right?
Saito: I'm not MAKING them. They're the ones who decided to call me that.
Ema: What...? So then... you weren't hired specifically as a producer?
Saito: As it stands, my nominal title is 'back of house'. Though, I admit I only applied because I saw the phrase 'seeking creative types' on the flyer.
Ema: Seeking creative types... What part of the job is creative, exactly?
Saito: Y'know, planning special events, designing butler merchandise, that kinda thing. Oh, and character roles, I s'pose.
Ema: Ohhh...
*Come to think of it, he was really passionate about me performing my role... I wonder if he directs all the other butlers' acting, too.*
*...Too bad I totally sucked at it.*
Ema: The cafe's sales went up after you were hired, right?
Saito: Yeah, I guess. The one thing I'm most proud of is my idea to change our target audience to older women.
Ema: Older women? Why...?
Saito: That demographic tends to have a lot of excess money and free time, see. And man, do they love dropping cash like it's burning a hole in their pocket.
Ema: I... I see...
*Uh... Did his eyes just start sparkling? Good grief, he sure loves to talk about money...*
Saito: Say what you like, but these older ladies buy 8x10 glossies like there's no tomorrow. To think we could turn a profit on selfies! Cha-ching!
Ema: ...
*So he's just pushing butler photos on all the customers... Hmm... That business model sounds familiar...*
Ema: Wait, what? H-Hold on a second. Are you going to sell photos of me, too...?
Saito: No, no. I don't sell 'em without the person's consent. But more than that, selling pics of you here might cause your market value to drop later!
Ema: I see...
*At least he's treating me differently from the others... I guess...*
*Still...*
Ema: ...Saito-san, you really love producing people, don't you?
Saito: I don't know that I LOVE it. It's just that nothing else feels worthwhile. More than anything, not doing it just feels so frustrating, y'know?
Ema: Frustrating how...?
Saito: Like if you see some limp-dick old man trying to promote an idol or whatever. Makes you think 'even I could do that,' y'know?
Ema: Sure... I guess I could see that...
Saito: ...That said, the whole reason I got into it in the first place is 'cuz supposedly it's what my old man used to do for a living, but then he passed away when I was still in diapers.
Ema: Oh... Is that so...?
*I, uh... wasn't expecting this conversation to take that turn...*
Saito: ...Don't tell anyone about that, okay? I haven't even told Miki.
Ema: That you used to wear diapers?
Saito: NO! I mean about my dad! Obviously I used to wear diapers! What's so crazy about that?!
Ema: I mean... I just can't picture it, that's all...
Saito: Ugh, I never should've said anything... Now I made this all weird and shit...
*Meh... I doubt I could use it to blackmail him, so I might as well keep it to myself.*
*Wait... But... If his father was working in a similar field, then maybe that means...*
Ema: What's with the weird look? Do I got some lint on me or something?
Ema: EEEK?! N-No, that's not it...! S-Say, umm... Just wondering, but why not make that Mao-san person into a model instead of me?
Saito: Mao-san, huh...
Ema: I mean, she's tall, and I'm pretty sure she's got her life together better than I do, so...
Saito: ...You wanna quit being a model?
Ema: N-No, that's not what I'm saying... Just... objectively speaking...
Saito: She wouldn't inspire Miki to make clothes.
Ema: Ohhh... That's true...
Saito: Anyway... Sorry to say, it just wouldn't work out with her.
Ema: Because of her age...?
Saito: Mention her age in front of her and she'll kick your ass, twerp. Anyway, that part aside, you and her are fundamentally different.
*Fundamentally different...? Let's see...*
Ema: ...Are slanted eyes really that popular overseas...?
Saito: That's not what I'm talking about.
Ema: ???
*Then what's so different about me? Sure, I'm a little taller, but Mao-san seems like the type who excels at just about anything...*
Saito: Haahh... Yeah, you REALLY need to work on your confidence. Tomorrow, you're on your own! That oughta help you build some character.
Ema: UuuuggggggghhhhHHHHHH...!
Saito: Could you stop wailing like a goddamn banshee?
Ema: If I was a banshee, you'd be dead by now...
*Tomorrow's going to be hell, I just know it...*
*......*
*...*
====================C13a_私がモテて何が悪い.txt====================
Ema: Miki-san... What's taking you so long...?
Man: What the... -san?
Ema: Huh...?
*That guy... I think I've seen him around, but I'm not sure...*
*I mean, I've never spoken to any of the guys at my school. Well, other than those two, of course...*
Man: Didn't expect to see you here! That yukata looks great on you!
Ema: Th... Thanks...
*Oh... It's all coming back to me now.*
*This guy is in my class... In fact, I think we sat next to each other at one point... I wanna say his name is... Takei-kun...?*
*Yeah! I remember being really jealous of his utterly average height, face, and personality...*
Takei: Me and my friends have been talking about you a lot lately.
Ema: Oh... I just want to say that I'm truly sorry for the huge scene I caused...
Takei: Huh...? Don't apologize! You're gonna make me feel bad... Though, I WAS pretty surprised to hear that you're friends with Shinjou and Hiraizumi.
Ema: ...
Takei: Well, I guess it wasn't all THAT surprising. I mean, you kinda... got a lot prettier over summer break...
Ema: What...?
Takei: To be fair, I didn't really look at your face much before... I mean, you'd always leave school as fast as possible...
Ema: W-Well, I, umm...
Takei: The girls in our class are totally jealous, but don't worry about them. I think you should talk to us more. We want to know more about you, you know?
Ema: O-Okay...
*What do I do...?*
*I've never had a classmate talk to me this way before... I have no idea how to react...*
Takei: Say... I'm actually here with a bunch of guys from our class. Would you wanna walk around with us? I'm sure they'd all be happy to see you.
Ema: With you...?
*I'm not sure about this... I'm not great at walking around in a large group...*
*But the whole reason I came to this night fair is to learn to be a normie, right?*
*If hanging out with a bunch of my classmates isn't being a normie, I don't know what is.*
Ema: Well, um, I'm actually here with someone, so if he could come too...
Takei: Of course! Totally!
Ema: Thank you. I think he should be back any moment now...
*Wow... "I'm here with someone"... That's such a normie thing to say...*
*You know, I feel like I'm really getting the hang of this "normie" thing! I really owe it all to Miki-san!*
*...*
Ema: He's still not back... I'm really sorry about this...
Takei: Oh, it's fine. I can wait.
*Miki-san said he was going to the bathroom... Maybe there was a huge line or something...*
*But still, men don't even need to sit down to take care of their business, right? It's been too long...*
Ema: Maybe he's gotten lost... I wouldn't put it past him...
*Maybe he thought of a new design on his way back, and then he got distracted and lost track of where he was...*
*I'm worried that he's by himself, helpless and afraid...*
Ema: I know! I saw a booth where we can report lost children. Maybe we should go there.
Takei: Uhhh, I don't think that'll help...
Ema: Of course it will! Even a guy like him would notice a loudspeaker announcement!
Takei: No, uh, that's not what I mean...
Ema: Or if that's not enough, we could hold up a sign that says 'Looking for Hiraizumi-kun' or something...
Miki: Stop.
Ema: Oh...!
*Miki-san's back...*
*He looks kinda upset. I wonder what happened. Did he step in dog poop on his way here? I wouldn't put THAT past him, either.*
Miki: A sign? What are you thinking? Where did that idea even come from?
Ema: Ohh, I get it. I should have just texted you! I'm sorry. I'm not used to this yet...
Miki: That's not it. You're treating me like a kid. Missing child announcement? That's kinda offensive, even coming from you.
Ema: I see... So that's what you were upset about...
*Wait... Was he listening to our whole conversation...?*
Ema: Have you been here the whole time? Why didn't you say anything?
Miki: Well...
Takei: Hey, it's not important. As long as you guys found each other again.
Miki: ...Who are you, exactly?
Takei: Oh, I'm Takei. I'm -san's classmate.
Ema: Oh, so you ARE Takei-kun. That's a relief. I'd feel bad if I got my own classmate's name wrong.
Takei: Ouch! That's so mean! Well, now you have an excuse to remember it.
Ema: Yeah, I'll definitely remember it now.
Miki: ...
Takei: Oh, sorry for derailing the convo. Anyway, as I was saying, would you guys wanna hang with us? The guys would be stoked to chill with you, Hiraizumi.
Ema: What should we do...?
Miki: Don't ask me. It's up to you what you want to do.
Ema: Okay... I was on the fence about it, but I think it'd be pretty normie of us to hang out in a group.
Takei: Weeell, I don't know if we're NORMIES, per se. None of us have girlfriends, y'know.
Ema: Is that an important factor?
Miki: Like I said, don't ask me. I don't care if it's 'normie' or not. I told you, it's up to you... This is so ridiculous!
Ema: Huh...?
*Miki-san is still upset... I didn't think he was the type to hold a grudge...*
*Maybe the dog poop was really warm and fresh, and it got everywhere...*
*But his sandals don't look dirty, so I guess not. Then... is he mad at me...?*
Ema: Umm... Did I do something wrong...?
Miki: ...
Takei: Uhhh... Sorry... I just realized you guys are on a date. Sorry for interrupting! I'm gonna go now.
Ema: Ah...!
*...*
*Takei-kun walked off... After he was nice enough to talk to me...*
Ema: ...What do I do...? Do you think I made him feel bad?
Miki: Well, maybe you should go after him, then. That's what you want to do, right?
Ema: B-But we have to stay together...
Miki: ...
Ema: (Ugh...)
*What's going on with him...? He's totally sulking...*
*I want to apologize, but I can't say sorry if I don't know what I did wrong...*
*I really don't know what to do...!*
*...*
====================C13b_プロデューサーのいうことは絶対.txt====================
*The second day of my long weekend awaits, and today I have the morning shift...*
Saito: Starting today, you'll be serving first-time customers. It's our job here to guide the uninitiated and help them to have a positive experience.
Ema: A positive experience? Easier said than done... Why should I give something like that to my customers when I've never even experienced it for myself?
Saito: Don't be selfish. And another thing! Today I wanna see you act like a real prince. Yesterday you broke character nonstop!
Mao: Hey, -chan... You've been requested...
Saito: Whoa, holy shit! It's only your second day and you already have a fan?
Mao: I'm... not sure she's a FAN, per se...
Older Lady: Now then, it's time for your daily lessons.
Ema: GYAAHH!!!
*The lady from yesterday is BACK?!*
Saito: Hmm... Maybe we should rebrand as a butler training cafe. That would be interesting.
Mao: Uh, I think that's a little too niche...
*...*
Ema: I... I'll pour your coffee now, young mistress.
Customer A: Hey, Mr. Butler! How can I get big?
Ema: Well... uhh... News coverage, I suppose...?
Customer A: I don't mean famous... I'm talking about my body.
Ema: Hmm, good question... Quit your diet and eat a bunch of calories, maybe? That sounds nice. I wish I were in your shoes right now.
Customer A: Not big as in fat, silly! I mean my height! Gosh, you're so funny! Totally hilarious!
Ema: Thanks...
*I just wish I understood which part of this conversation was supposed to be a joke...*
*Whatever... As long as she's happy, I guess...*
*...*
Mao: -chan's a big hit, huh? People are asking about her on the forums now!
Saito: ...
Mao: I guess the customers really like the way her natural cluelessness peeks through her chilly outer facade.
Saito: ...Then this is pointless...
Mao: Hmm...?
Saito: ! Come here for a sec.
Ema: Y-Yes?
*What's going on...? Saito-san's got this scary look on his face... Did I piss him off again...?*
Ema: Umm... Whatever I did, I'm sorry...
Saito: I haven't even started talking yet.
Ema: Well, you seem mad, so...
Saito: Yeah, I'd say I'm definitely mad. I told you to act like a PRINCE. I'm not saying you have to be good at it, but why the hell aren't you at least TRYING?
Ema: Well... I just can't picture what a prince is supposed to be like. Can't I just wipe my forehead sweat with a handkerchief and call it done?
Saito: I'd make a retort, but I don't even GET that reference!
Ema: I think he's a baseball player.
Saito: There's a prince of baseball? For real?
Ema: Everyone calls him that. Apparently he got into a great college, but I saw on the news that all his pitches were home runs, so now he's a full-time batting-practice pitcher or something.
Saito: Hmmm... He's working a job like that even though he got into a good college? He must really love baseball.
Mao: Guys, we're getting off topic...
Saito: Fuck, you're right! Anyway, I don't want some obscure prince like that! I want you to be an ACTUAL prince—
Ema: Then just let me be a singing idol prince...
Saito: Like I said, I don't get any of these references!
Mao: Oh my god, I feel so old...
Saito: Listen. What I want is a 'knight in shining armor' type. Every girl wants a gallant hero to ride in on a white horse and rescue her, right? Now let's see you try to act it out as best you can.
Ema: B-But... if I tried to act like that, I'm pretty sure everyone would just laugh at me...
Saito: Let 'em laugh. Customers come here wanting to step into a different world, so who cares. Let 'em have a good time.
Ema: Okay... but I still don't really understand... Could you show me an example?
Mao: Yeah, she's right! You can't expect her to get it just by telling her. You have to SHOW her!
Saito: Nice try, but that's not gonna work on me! And as for the rest of you, it's not happening, so quit looking at me like that!
Ema: ...Tch...
Saito: Don't you cluck your tongue at me, missy. Quit making excuses and do your job.
Mao: Listen, Producer, don't you think she's fine the way she is? I mean, the customers seem to like her.
Saito: No, she's NOT fine that way. I'm telling you, she'll be more popular as a prince, guaranteed.
Mao: But she doesn't want to...
Saito: Doesn't matter if she WANTS to. We're doing this to help her build character.
Mao: I get what you're saying, but maybe you could try to phrase things a little more?
Saito: Just SHUT UP! This is between me and her! You aren't involved in this, so BUTT OUT!!!
Mao: I'm not involved? Wow. I'm an employee here too, you know.
Saito: Oh... I... I apologize... I went too far...
*What the... Did Saito-san just cave to her? Now THAT'S a first... at least that I've seen...*
Saito: ...I'm gonna go calm myself down.
Mao: All right. See you when you get back.
Mao: Whew... I gotta say, I've never seen him get quite that emotional before.
Ema: Are you kidding? He wants me to be this 'gallant prince,' but if you ask me, he's grumpier than an old zebra mare...
Mao: Really? Here at the cafe, he's usually pretty chill. In fact, one time I tried to piss him off on purpose by lacing his food with shichimi pepper as a prank, but he just shrugged it off.
Ema: If I did that, he'd totally kick my ass.
Mao: Apparently he lives with his mom, so maybe she raised him to be nice to women?
Ema: Hmm... But in that case...
*That sounds nothing like the Saito-san I know. Everyone here seems to think he's this super nice guy, but he's a total asshole when he's around me...*
*So that must mean...*
Ema: ...In other words, Saito-san doesn't see me as a woman, then.
Mao: Oh, no, uhh... I didn't mean it like that...
